    ) AS [t1]
    WHERE [t1].[row] <= 1
) AS [t2] ON [t].[MangaId] = [t2].[MangaId]

    OFFSET @__p_1 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
